\"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements\nusing idx_t = uint64_t;\nusing val_t = double;\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Update all elements\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];\n            const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[i];\n            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n            \n            // Start with external flow\n            val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n            \n            // Add flux from all connected elements\n            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];\n                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n            }\n            \n            // Update element state\n            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n        }\n        \n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n \n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    printf(\"Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"============================================\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %d x %d = %d elements\\n\", n_elems_root, n_elems_root, n_elems);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", n_iters);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : the elements are filled in explicitly.\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U*) noexcept {\n        static_assert(std::is_trivially_default_constructible_v<U>);\n    }\n    template <typename U, typename... the mesh setup\n// and the simulation use exactly the same distribution (NUMA first touch).\ninline void threadRange(size_t n, size_t& begin, size_t& end) {\n    constexpr size_t grain = CACHE_LINE / sizeof(ElementDynamic);\n    const size_t n_threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n    const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n    const size_t n_blocks = (n + grain - 1) / grain;\n    const size_t per_thread = n_blocks / n_threads;\n    const size_t remainder = n_blocks % n_threads;\n    const size_t first_block = tid * per_thread + std::min(tid, remainder);\n    const size_t n_own = per_thread + (tid < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n\n    begin = std::min(first_block * grain, n);\n    end = std::min((first_block + n_own) * grain, n);\n}\n\n// Material computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstddef>\n#include <cstdint>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <new>\n#include <type_traits>\n#include <utility>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements\nusing idx_t = uint64_t;\nusing val_t = double;\n\n// Cache line size used for alignment and false-sharing avoidance\nconstexpr size_t CACHE_LINE = 64;\n\n// Allocator that leaves default-constructed (trivial) elements uninitialized.\n// This keeps std::vector::resize() from touching every page from the calling\n// thread, so that the parallel initialization loops below perform the NUMA\n// first touch and every thread ends up owning the pages it later works on.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct NoInitAllocator {\n    using value_type = T;\n\n    NoInitAllocator() noexcept = default;\n    template <typename U>\n    constexpr NoInitAllocator(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) noexcept {}\n\n    T* allocate(size_t n) {\n        return static_cast<T*>(::operator new(n * sizeof(T), std::align_val_t(CACHE_LINE)));\n    }\n    void deallocate(T* p, size_t) noexcept {\n        ::operator delete(p, std::align_val_t(CACHE_LINE));\n    }\n\n    // Skip value-initialization; the elements are filled in explicitly.\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U*) noexcept {\n        static_assert(std::is_trivially_default_constructible_v<U>);\n    }\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator==(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return true; }\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator!=(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return false; }\n};\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing ElemVector = std::vector<T, NoInitAllocator<T>>;\n\nusing DynamicArray = ElemVector<ElementDynamic>;\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    ElemVector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    DynamicArray elements_dynamic;\n    DynamicArray elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Split [0, n) into one contiguous chunk per thread of the enclosing parallel\n// region. Chunk boundaries are cache line aligned with respect to\n// ElementDynamic so that neighbouring threads never write the same line, and\n// the partition depends only on the thread count, which lets the mesh setup\n// and the simulation use exactly the same distribution (NUMA first touch).\ninline void threadRange(size_t n, size_t& begin, size_t& end) {\n    constexpr size_t grain = CACHE_LINE / sizeof(ElementDynamic);\n    const size_t n_threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n    const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n    const size_t n_blocks = (n + grain - 1) / grain;\n    const size_t per_thread = n_blocks / n_threads;\n    const size_t remainder = n_blocks % n_threads;\n    const size_t first_block = tid * per_thread + std::min(tid, remainder);\n    const size_t n_own = per_thread + (tid < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n\n    begin = std::min(first_block * grain, n);\n    end = std::min((first_block + n_own) * grain, n);\n}\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize elements and build connectivity in parallel. Each thread owns\n    // exactly the index range it will later update in the simulation, so this\n    // loop performs the NUMA first touch of the pages it will be working on.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        size_t begin, end;\n        threadRange(static_cast<size_t>(n_elems), begin, end);\n\n        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            const int x = static_cast<int>(i) / n_elems_root;\n            const int y = static_cast<int>(i) % n_elems_root;\n\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n            elem.material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n            elem.num_connections = 0;\n            world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Update all elements\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];\n            const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[i];\n            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n            \n            // Start with external flow\n            val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n            \n            // Add flux from all connected elements\n            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];\n                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n            }\n            \n            // Update element state\n            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n        }\n        \n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) the elements are filled in explicitly.\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U*) noexcept {\n        static_assert(std::is_trivially_default_constructible_v<U>);\n    }\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator==(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return true; }\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator!=(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return false; }\n};\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing ElemVector = std::vector<T, NoInitAllocator<T>>;\n\nusing DynamicArray = ElemVector<ElementDynamic>;\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    ElemVector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    DynamicArray elements_dynamic;\n    DynamicArray elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Split [0, n) into one contiguous chunk per thread of the enclosing parallel\n// region. Chunk boundaries are cache line aligned with respect to\n// ElementDynamic so that neighbouring threads never write the same line, and\n// the partition depends only on the thread count, which lets the mesh setup\n// and the simulation use exactly the same distribution (NUMA first touch).\ninline void threadRange(size_t n, size_t& begin, size_t& end) {\n    constexpr size_t grain = CACHE_LINE / sizeof(ElementDynamic);\n    const size_t n_threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n    const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n    const size_t n_blocks = (n + grain - 1) / grain;\n    const size_t per_thread = n_blocks / n_threads;\n    const size_t remainder = n_blocks % n_threads;\n    const size_t first_block = tid * per_thread + std::min(tid, remainder);\n    const size_t n_own = per_thread + (tid < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n\n    begin = std::min(first_block * grain, n);\n    end = std::min((first_block + n_own) * grain, n);\n}\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize elements and build connectivity in parallel. Each thread owns\n    // exactly the index range it will later update in the simulation, so this\n    // loop performs the NUMA first touch of the pages it will be working on.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        size_t begin, end;\n        threadRange(static_cast<size_t>(n_elems), begin, end);\n\n        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            const int x = static_cast<int>(i) / n_elems_root;\n            const int y = static_cast<int>(i) % n_elems_root;\n\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n            elem.material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n            elem.num_connections = 0;\n            world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const ElementStatic* const __restrict elems_static = world.elements_static.data();\n    const Material* const __restrict materials = world.materials.data();\n\n    // Buffers are flipped by pointer instead of by swapping the vectors, so the\n    // parallel region can stay open across all iterations.\n    ElementDynamic* read_buf = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n    ElementDynamic* write_buf = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // Same partitioning as the mesh setup: every thread updates the range\n        // of elements whose pages it first touched.\n        size_t begin, end;\n        threadRange(n_elems, begin, end);\n\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n            const ElementDynamic* const __restrict src = read_buf;\n            ElementDynamic* const __restrict dst = write_buf;\n\n            // Update all elements owned by this thread\n            for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n                const ElementStatic& elem_static = elems_static[i];\n                const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = src[i];\n                const Material& mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n                // Start with external flow\n                val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n                // Add flux from all connected elements\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                    const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                    const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = src[neighbor_idx];\n                    total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n                }\n\n                // Update element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = dst[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n\n            // All updates must be visible before the buffers are flipped\n            #pragma omp barrier\n            #pragma omp bit-exact\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(^ : hash) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const Combined with\n// the first-touch initialization this keeps both memory controllers busy,\n// which is what this bandwidth-bound kernel needs. SMT siblings come last.\n// Returns the number of distinct physical cores available.\nsize_t buildCpuOrder(std::vector<int>& order) {\n    order.clear();\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    struct CpuInfo {\n        int smt_rank; <sched.h>\n#endif\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements\nusing idx_t = uint64_t;\nusing val_t = double;\n\n// Cache line size used for alignment and false-sharing avoidance\nconstexpr size_t CACHE_LINE = 64;\n\n// Allocator that leaves default-constructed (trivial) elements uninitialized.\n// This keeps std::vector::resize() from touching every page from the calling\n// thread, so that the parallel initialization loops below perform the NUMA\n// first touch and every thread ends up owning the pages it later works on.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct NoInitAllocator {\n    using value_type = T;\n\n    NoInitAllocator() noexcept = default;\n    template <typename U>\n    constexpr NoInitAllocator(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) noexcept {}\n\n    T* allocate(size_t n) {\n        return static_cast<T*>(::operator new(n * sizeof(T), std::align_val_t(CACHE_LINE)));\n    }\n    void deallocate(T* p, size_t) noexcept {\n        ::operator delete(p, std::align_val_t(CACHE_LINE));\n    }\n\n    // Skip value-initialization; the elements are filled in explicitly.\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U*) noexcept {\n        static_assert(std::is_trivially_default_constructible_v<U>);\n    }\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator==(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return true; }\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator!=(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return false; }\n};\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing ElemVector = std::vector<T, NoInitAllocator<T>>;\n\nusing DynamicArray = ElemVector<ElementDynamic>;\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    ElemVector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    DynamicArray elements_dynamic;\n    DynamicArray elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Split [0, n) into one contiguous chunk per thread of the enclosing parallel\n// region. Chunk boundaries are cache line aligned with respect to\n// ElementDynamic so that neighbouring threads never write the same line, and\n// the partition depends only on the thread count, which lets the mesh setup\n// and the simulation use exactly the same distribution (NUMA first touch).\ninline void threadRange(size_t n, size_t& begin, size_t& end) {\n    constexpr size_t grain = CACHE_LINE / sizeof(ElementDynamic);\n    const size_t n_threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n    const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n    const size_t n_blocks = (n + grain - 1) / grain;\n    const size_t per_thread = n_blocks / n_threads;\n    const size_t remainder = n_blocks % n_threads;\n    const size_t first_block = tid * per_thread + std::min(tid, remainder);\n    const size_t n_own = per_thread + (tid < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n\n    begin = std::min(first_block * grain, n);\n    end = std::min((first_block + n_own) * grain, n);\n}\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize elements and build connectivity in parallel. Each thread owns\n    // exactly the index range it will later update in the simulation, so this\n    // loop performs the NUMA first touch of the pages it will be working on.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        size_t begin, end;\n        threadRange(static_cast<size_t>(n_elems), begin, end);\n\n        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            const int x = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const world.materials.data();\n\n    // Buffers are flipped by pointer instead of by swapping the vectors, so the\n    // parallel region can stay open across all iterations.\n    ElementDynamic* read_buf = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n    ElementDynamic* write_buf = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // Same partitioning as the mesh setup: every thread elements\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                    const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                    const elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n\n            // All updates must be visible before the buffers are flipped\n            #pragma omp barrier\n            #pragma omp std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const DynamicArray& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    // XOR is associative and commutative, so the reduction is bit-exact\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(^ : hash) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const Chunk boundaries are cache line aligned with respect to\n// ElementDynamic so that neighbouring threads never write the same line, and\n// the partition depends only on the thread count, which lets the mesh setup\n// and the simulation use exactly the same distribution (NUMA first touch).\ninline void threadRange(size_t n, size_t& begin, size_t& end) {\n    constexpr size_t grain = CACHE_LINE / sizeof(ElementDynamic);\n    const size_t n_threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n    const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n    const size_t n_blocks = (n + grain - 1) / grain;\n    const size_t per_thread = n_blocks / n_threads;\n    const size_t remainder = n_blocks % n_threads;\n    const size_t first_block = tid * per_thread + std::min(tid, remainder);\n    const size_t n_own = per_thread + (tid < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n\n    begin = std::min(first_block * grain, n);\n    end = std::min((first_block + n_own) * grain, n);\n}\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize elements and build connectivity in parallel. Each thread owns\n    // exactly the index range it will later update in the simulation, so this\n    // loop performs the NUMA first touch of the pages it will be working on.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        size_t begin, end;\n        threadRange(static_cast<size_t>(n_elems), begin, end);\n\n        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            const int x = static_cast<int>(i) / n_elems_root;\n            const int y = static_cast<int>(i) % n_elems_root;\n\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n            elem.material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n            elem.num_connections = 0;\n            world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const ElementStatic* const __restrict elems_static = world.elements_static.data();\n    const Material* const __restrict materials = world.materials.data();\n\n    // Buffers are flipped by pointer instead of by swapping the vectors, so the\n    // parallel region can stay open across all iterations.\n    ElementDynamic* read_buf = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n    ElementDynamic* write_buf = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // Same partitioning as the mesh setup: every thread updates the range\n        // of elements whose pages it first touched.\n        size_t begin, end;\n        threadRange(n_elems, begin, end);\n\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n            const ElementDynamic* const __restrict src = read_buf;\n            ElementDynamic* const __restrict dst = write_buf;\n\n            // Update all elements owned by this thread\n            for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n                const ElementStatic& elem_static = elems_static[i];\n                const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = src[i];\n                const Material& mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n                // Start with external flow\n                val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n                // Add flux from all connected elements\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                    const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                    const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = src[neighbor_idx];\n                    total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n                }\n\n                // Update element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = dst[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n\n            // All updates must be visible before the buffers are flipped\n            #pragma omp barrier\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                std::swap(read_buf, write_buf);\n            }\n            // implicit barrier at the end of 'single'\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make sure the freshest state lives in world.elements_dynamic\n    if (read_buf != world.elements_dynamic.data()) {\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if bit-exact\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(^ : hash) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr parseCpuList(const char* path) {\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (!f) {\n        return cpus;\n    }\n    char buf[1024];\n    const bool ok = fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr;\n    fclose(f);\n    if (!ok) {\n        return cpus;\n    }\n\n    const char* p = buf;\n    while (*p) {\n        char* endp = nullptr;\n        long first = strtol(p, Combined with\n// the first-touch initialization this keeps both memory controllers busy,\n// which is what this bandwidth-bound kernel needs. SMT siblings come last.\n// Returns the number of distinct physical cores available.\nsize_t buildCpuOrder(std::vector<int>& order) {\n    order.clear();\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n Chunk boundaries are cache line aligned with respect to\n// ElementDynamic so that neighbouring threads never write the same line, and\n// the partition depends only on the thread count, which lets the mesh setup\n// and the simulation use exactly the same distribution (NUMA first touch).\ninline void threadRange(size_t n, size_t& begin, size_t& end) {\n    constexpr size_t grain = CACHE_LINE / sizeof(ElementDynamic);\n    const size_t n_threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n    const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n    const size_t n_blocks = (n + grain - 1) / grain;\n    const size_t per_thread = n_blocks / n_threads;\n    const size_t remainder = n_blocks % n_threads;\n    const size_t first_block = tid * per_thread + std::min(tid, remainder);\n    const size_t n_own = per_thread + (tid < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n\n    begin = std::min(first_block * grain, n);\n    end = std::min((first_block + n_own) * grain, n);\n}\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize elements and build connectivity in parallel. static_cast<int>(i) / n_elems_root;\n            const int y = static_cast<int>(i) % n_elems_root;\n\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n            elem.material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n            elem.num_connections = 0;\n            world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const ElementStatic* const __restrict elems_static = world.elements_static.data();\n    const Material* const __restrict materials = world.materials.data();\n\n    // Buffers are flipped by pointer instead of by swapping the vectors, so the\n    // parallel region can stay open across all iterations.\n    ElementDynamic* read_buf = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n    ElementDynamic* write_buf = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // Same partitioning as the mesh setup: every thread updates the range\n        // of elements whose pages it first touched.\n        size_t begin, end;\n        threadRange(n_elems, begin, end);\n\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n            const ElementDynamic* const __restrict src = read_buf;\n            ElementDynamic* const __restrict dst = write_buf;\n\n            // Update all elements owned by this thread\n            for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n                const ElementStatic& elem_static = elems_static[i];\n                const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = src[i];\n                const Material& mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n                // Start with external flow\n                val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n                // Add flux from all connected elements\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                    const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                    const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = src[neighbor_idx];\n                    total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n                }\n\n                // Update element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = dst[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n\n            // All updates must be visible before the buffers are flipped\n            #pragma omp barrier\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                std::swap(read_buf, write_buf);\n            }\n            // implicit barrier at the end of 'single'\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make sure the freshest state lives in world.elements_dynamic\n    if (read_buf != world.elements_dynamic.data()) {\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const DynamicArray& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    // XOR is associative and commutative, so the reduction is bit-exact\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(^ : hash) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n#ifdef __linux__\n// Read a \"0-3,8\" style CPU list from sysfs\nstd::vector<int> parseCpuList(const char* path) {\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (!f) {\n        return cpus;\n    }\n    char buf[1024];\n    const bool ok = fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr;\n    fclose(f);\n    if (!ok) {\n        return cpus;\n    }\n\n    const char* p = buf;\n    while (*p) {\n        char* endp = nullptr;\n        long first = strtol(p, &endp, 10);\n        if (endp == p) {\n            break;\n        }\n        p = endp;\n        long last = first;\n        if (*p == '-') {\n            last = strtol(p + 1, &endp, 10);\n            p = endp;\n        }\n        for (long c = first; c <= last; ++c) {\n            cpus.push_back(static_cast<int>(c));\n        }\n        if (*p == ',') {\n            ++p;\n        } else {\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    return cpus;\n}\n\n// Order the CPUs this process may run on so that consecutive OpenMP threads\n// land on distinct physical cores, alternating between sockets. Combined with\n// the first-touch initialization this keeps both memory controllers busy,\n// which is what this bandwidth-bound kernel needs. SMT siblings come last.\n// Returns the number of distinct physical cores available.\nsize_t buildCpuOrder(std::vector<int>& order) {\n    order.clear();\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    struct CpuInfo {\n        int smt_rank;  // position among the usable siblings of its core\n        int package;\n        int cpu;\n    };\n    std::vector<CpuInfo> infos;\n    int max_package = 0;\n\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        char path[256];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                 \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", cpu);\n        const std::vector<int> siblings = parseCpuList(path);\n        int smt_rank = 0;\n        for (const int sib : siblings) {\n            if (sib == cpu) {\n                break;\n            }\n            if (CPU_ISSET(sib, &allowed)) {\n                ++smt_rank;\n            }\n        }\n\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                 \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        int package = 0;\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &package) != 1) {\n                package = 0;\n            }\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        package = std::max(package, 0);\n        max_package = std::max(max_package, package);\n\n        infos.push_back(CpuInfo{smt_rank, package, cpu});\n    }\n\n    if (infos.empty()) {\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    std::sort(infos.begin(), infos.end(), [](const CpuInfo& a, const CpuInfo& b) {\n        if (a.smt_rank != b.smt_rank) return a.smt_rank < b.smt_rank;\n        if (a.package != b.package) return a.package < b.package;\n        return a.cpu < b.cpu;\n    });\n\n    // Round-robin over the packages within each SMT level\n    const size_t n_packages = static_cast<size_t>(max_package) + 1;\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> buckets(n_packages);\n    size_t n_cores = 0;\n    int smt_rank = infos.front().smt_rank;\n\n    auto flush_buckets = [&]() {\n        for (size_t idx = 0, emitted = 1; emitted > 0; ++idx) {\n            emitted = 0;\n            for (const auto& bucket : buckets) {\n                if (idx < bucket.size()) {\n                    order.push_back(bucket[idx]);\n                    ++emitted;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        for (auto& bucket : buckets) {\n            bucket.clear();\n        }\n    };\n\n    for (const CpuInfo& info : infos) {\n        if (info.smt_rank != smt_rank) {\n            flush_buckets();\n            smt_rank = info.smt_rank;\n        }\n        if (info.smt_rank == 0) {\n            ++n_cores;\n        }\n        buckets[static_cast<size_t>(info.package)].push_back(info.cpu);\n    }\n    flush_buckets();\n\n    return n_cores;\n}\n#endif  // __linux__\n\n// Pin the OpenMP threads unless the user asked for a specific placement.\nvoid configureThreadPlacement() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n throughput: default to one thread per physical core.\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(n_cores));\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(order[tid % order.size()], &set);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n#endif\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"    const int n_measured_iters = std::max(n_iters - 1, 1);\n    const double time_per_iter = static_cast<double>(duration_ms) / n_measured_iters;\n    const double giga_elems_per_sec = the elements are filled in explicitly.\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U*) noexcept {\n        static_assert(std::is_trivially_default_constructible_v<U>);\n    }\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator==(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return true; }\n    template <typename U>\n    bool operator!=(const NoInitAllocator<U>&) const noexcept { return false; }\n};\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing ElemVector = std::vector<T, NoInitAllocator<T>>;\n\nusing DynamicArray = ElemVector<ElementDynamic>;\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    ElemVector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    DynamicArray elements_dynamic;\n    DynamicArray elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Split [0, n) into one contiguous chunk per thread of the enclosing parallel\n// region. Chunk boundaries are cache line aligned with respect to\n// ElementDynamic so that neighbouring threads never write the same line, and\n// the partition depends only on the thread count, which lets the mesh setup\n// and the simulation use exactly the same distribution (NUMA first touch).\ninline void threadRange(size_t n, size_t& begin, size_t& end) {\n    constexpr size_t grain = CACHE_LINE / sizeof(ElementDynamic);\n    const size_t n_threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n    const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n\n    const size_t n_blocks = (n + grain - 1) / grain;\n    const size_t per_thread = n_blocks / n_threads;\n    const size_t remainder = n_blocks % n_threads;\n    const size_t first_block = tid * per_thread + std::min(tid, remainder);\n    const size_t n_own = per_thread + (tid < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n\n    begin = std::min(first_block * grain, n);\n    end = std::min((first_block + n_own) * grain, n);\n}\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize elements and build connectivity in parallel. Each thread owns\n    // exactly the index range it will later update in the simulation, so this\n    // loop performs the NUMA first touch of the pages it will be working on.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        size_t begin, end;\n        threadRange(static_cast<size_t>(n_elems), begin, end);\n\n        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            const int x = static_cast<int>(i) / n_elems_root;\n            const int y = static_cast<int>(i) % n_elems_root;\n\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n            elem.material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n            elem.num_connections = 0;\n            world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n            world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const ElementStatic* const __restrict elems_static = world.elements_static.data();\n    const Material* const __restrict materials = world.materials.data();\n\n    // Buffers are flipped by pointer instead of by swapping the vectors, so the\n    // parallel region can stay open across all iterations.\n    ElementDynamic* read_buf = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n    ElementDynamic* write_buf = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // Same partitioning as the mesh setup: every thread updates the range\n        // of elements whose pages it first touched.\n        size_t begin, end;\n        threadRange(n_elems, begin, end);\n\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n            const ElementDynamic* const __restrict src = read_buf;\n            ElementDynamic* const __restrict dst = write_buf;\n\n            // Update all elements owned by this thread\n            for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n                const ElementStatic& elem_static = elems_static[i];\n                const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = src[i];\n                const Material& mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n                // Start with external flow\n                val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n                // Add flux from all connected elements\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                    const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                    const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = src[neighbor_idx];\n                    total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n                }\n\n                // Update element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = dst[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n\n            // All updates must be visible before the buffers are flipped\n            #pragma omp barrier\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                std::swap(read_buf, write_buf);\n            }\n            // implicit barrier at the end of 'single'\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make sure the freshest state lives in world.elements_dynamic\n    if (read_buf != world.elements_dynamic.data()) {\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const DynamicArray& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    // XOR is associative and commutative, so the reduction is bit-exact\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(^ : hash) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n#ifdef __linux__\n// Read a \"0-3,8\" style CPU list from sysfs\nstd::vector<int> parseCpuList(const char* path) {\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (!f) {\n        return cpus;\n    }\n    char buf[1024];\n    const bool ok = fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr;\n    fclose(f);\n    if (!ok) {\n        return cpus;\n    }\n\n    const char* p = buf;\n    while (*p) {\n        char* endp = nullptr;\n        long first = strtol(p, &endp, 10);\n        if (endp == p) {\n            break;\n        }\n        p = endp;\n        long last = first;\n        if (*p == '-') {\n            last = strtol(p + 1, &endp, 10);\n            p = endp;\n        }\n        for (long c = first; c <= last; ++c) {\n            cpus.push_back(static_cast<int>(c));\n        }\n        if (*p == ',') {\n            ++p;\n        } else {\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    return cpus;\n}\n\n// Order the CPUs this process may run on so that consecutive OpenMP threads\n// land on distinct physical cores, alternating between sockets. Combined with\n// the first-touch initialization this keeps both memory controllers busy,\n// which is what this bandwidth-bound kernel needs. SMT siblings come last.\n// Returns the number of distinct physical cores available.\nsize_t buildCpuOrder(std::vector<int>& order) {\n    order.clear();\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    struct CpuInfo {\n        int smt_rank;  // position among the usable siblings of its core\n        int package;\n        int cpu;\n    };\n    std::vector<CpuInfo> infos;\n    int max_package = 0;\n\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        char path[256];\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                 \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", cpu);\n        const std::vector<int> siblings = parseCpuList(path);\n        int smt_rank = 0;\n        for (const int sib : siblings) {\n            if (sib == cpu) {\n                break;\n            }\n            if (CPU_ISSET(sib, &allowed)) {\n                ++smt_rank;\n            }\n        }\n\n        snprintf(path, sizeof(path),\n                 \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/physical_package_id\", cpu);\n        int package = 0;\n        if (FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\")) {\n            if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &package) != 1) {\n                package = 0;\n            }\n            fclose(f);\n        }\n        package = std::max(package, 0);\n        max_package = std::max(max_package, package);\n\n        infos.push_back(CpuInfo{smt_rank, package, cpu});\n    }\n\n    if (infos.empty()) {\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    std::sort(infos.begin(), infos.end(), [](const CpuInfo& a, const CpuInfo& b) {\n        if (a.smt_rank != b.smt_rank) return a.smt_rank < b.smt_rank;\n        if (a.package != b.package) return a.package < b.package;\n        return a.cpu < b.cpu;\n    });\n\n    // Round-robin over the packages within each SMT level\n    const size_t n_packages = static_cast<size_t>(max_package) + 1;\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> buckets(n_packages);\n    size_t n_cores = 0;\n    int smt_rank = infos.front().smt_rank;\n\n    auto flush_buckets = [&]() {\n        for (size_t idx = 0, emitted = 1; emitted > 0; ++idx) {\n            emitted = 0;\n            for (const auto& bucket : buckets) {\n                if (idx < bucket.size()) {\n                    order.push_back(bucket[idx]);\n                    ++emitted;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        for (auto& bucket : buckets) {\n            bucket.clear();\n        }\n    };\n\n    for (const CpuInfo& info : infos) {\n        if (info.smt_rank != smt_rank) {\n            flush_buckets();\n            smt_rank = info.smt_rank;\n        }\n        if (info.smt_rank == 0) {\n            ++n_cores;\n        }\n        buckets[static_cast<size_t>(info.package)].push_back(info.cpu);\n    }\n    flush_buckets();\n\n    return n_cores;\n}\n#endif  // __linux__\n\n// Pin the OpenMP threads unless the user asked for a specific placement.\nvoid configureThreadPlacement() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr) {\n        return;  // respect the user's placement policy\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    const size_t n_cores = buildCpuOrder(order);\n    if (order.empty() || n_cores == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // This kernel is memory bandwidth bound, so SMT siblings add contention\n    // rather than throughput: default to one thread per physical core.\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(n_cores));\n    }\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(order[tid % order.size()], &set);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n#endif\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"
